- **All code comments removed (user request, 2026-10-13; rule "B", whole app).** Rule from now on: **no comments in code** — only PHPDoc type lines (`@param`, `@return`, `@property`, `@var`) are kept; the "why" goes in the chat and in this handover. Done with a PHP-tokenizer script (real comments only, never `//` inside strings/URLs) over `app/`, `routes/`, `database/`, `resources/views/` (incl. Blade `{{-- --}}` and HTML `<!-- -->`), `tests/`: 161 of 292 files changed, ~1.080 lines removed, 70 type lines kept. **`config/` left untouched** (Laravel's own setting docs) — ask before stripping it. Checks after: all PHP files lint, all Blade views compile (`view:cache`), 40 app routes register, sales / Material Issue / Cancel WO tests re-run identical, 24 pages render. Before running, everything was `git add -A` staged as a restore point (no commit); **note: the user's "done commit" did not include Material Return, Production Receipt, Cancel WO, Sales etc. — last commit is `2e46ad1` (2026-10-09)**. Saved as a memory too.

- **SO fields extended to the user's spec (2026-10-13).** User's list: pelanggan, tanggal buat (disabled, always today), tanggal pengiriman, per line nama barang / kuantitas / satuan / harga jual, diskon (opsional), nama penjual, syarat pembayaran, biaya lainnya, tax to discuss. Decisions: **(1) discount = one amount on the whole order** (rupiah, ≤ products subtotal — the rupiah-vs-% choice was mine); **(2) salesperson = a user with the Sales role**, defaulting to the creator; **(3) payment terms = fixed list** `PaymentTerm` enum (cash/Tunai, NET 7/14/30/60, `days()`), due date computed later on the invoice; **(4) other charges = several lines on their own tab** ("later PO will be like this too"); **(5) no tax on the SO** (invoice/Accounting). Migration `sales/2026_10_13_120000_add_terms_discount_charges_to_sales_orders.php` (`salesperson_id` required FK users, `payment_terms`, `discount_amount`; new `sales_order_charges` description + amount). `total_amount` = products subtotal − discount + charges. `SalesOrder::salesperson()`, `charges()`, `itemsSubtotal()`, `chargesTotal()`; DTO + `SaveSalesOrderDraftAction` extended (salesperson must have the Sales role, discount ≤ subtotal → `InvalidSalesOrderException` NOT_A_SALESPERSON / DISCOUNT_TOO_HIGH). **New reusable `<x-tab-bar :tabs :label>`** (`resources/views/components/tab-bar.blade.php`): Flux free has no tabs (Pro only), so Alpine-based — reads/writes a `tab` variable from the parent `x-data="{ tab: $wire.entangle('activeTab') }"`, sliding accent underline (transform + width transition, reduced-motion respected), count pill per tab, red dot + sr-only text on a tab with errors, ←/→ keys, `role=tablist/tab`; panels use `x-show` + `x-cloak` (added `[x-cloak]{display:none!important}` to `resources/css/app.css`). SO form: customer, order date (disabled), delivery date, salesperson picker, payment terms picker, notes; summary card with products subtotal, discount input, other charges, total; tabs **Produk | Biaya Lainnya** ("Add charge" jumps to the charges tab; on save errors the form switches to the tab that has them). SO detail: payment terms + salesperson in the info block, value card with the breakdown, tabs **Produk | Biaya Lainnya | Pengiriman**. ERD updated (`payment_term` enum, new columns, `sales_order_charges`). 23 keys (695). Verified (rolled back): defaults, discount above subtotal refused, charge errors switch the tab, total 450.000 − 50.000 + 170.000 = 570.000 stored and shown, edit reloads everything, a Manager can't be the salesperson; the full SO → delivery flow still passes. (3A) **formulas are edited in place, versioning removed**: one formula per product; safe because every WO already copies its plan at creation, so editing a formula only affects new WOs.

Version text removed from WO create/list/show, the print page, `ProductionSeeder` and the model casts.

One exception `InvalidMasterDataException` (PRODUCT_LOCKED, SELLING_PRICE_NOT_FG, FORMULA_PRODUCT_TYPE, FORMULA_EXISTS, FORMULA_NO_MATERIALS, FORMULA_MATERIAL_TYPE, FORMULA_SELF, CATALOG_NOT_RAW).

Sidebar: Manajemen → Produk; Pembelian → Pemasok; Produksi → Formula Produksi.

One source for each piece: `ProductType::badgeClasses()` (replaces the copies `typeBadgeClasses()` in the products page and `productTypeChipClasses()` in WO create; formulas list uses it too) and **`Product::toPickerOption($meta, $disabledReason)`** (used by PO create, PO detail, supplier catalog, SO form, Material Issue extras, formula product + material pickers). `<x-picker>` draws an optional `badge` (`['label', 'class']`) after the title; its two leftover comments were removed. Right-hand meta kept where it carries other info (price/unit on PO, unit in the catalog, stock on SO / Material Issue); the formula pickers' meta was the type, now the badge. **PO detail "add item" switched from `flux:select` to `<x-picker>`** (`newItemOptions()`; products already on the PO shown disabled "Sudah dipilih"). Verified (rolled back): badges on all 7 pickers, no badge on customers/payment terms/work centers, PO detail price pre-fill + add still work. Visual layout not verified.
- **Bug fixed: sidebar "cut off" when a picker opened near the bottom (user report, 2026-10-13).** Cause: the `<x-picker>` panel opened downward, ran past the end of the page, and the browser grew the page to fit it; Flux's sticky sidebar only covers the page's own height, so the extra area had no sidebar. The old rule only flipped upward when the *whole* panel fit above. Now on open it measures the space below and above the trigger, opens toward the side with more room when it doesn't fit below, and caps the list height (`listMax`, 96–256 px) to that space, so the panel never leaves the window; `overscroll-contain` stops the page scrolling when the list hits its end. Visual check needed in the browser.
- **SO form counts reserved stock (user, 2026-10-13).** Each product row's hint is now "Stok X · Dipesan pelanggan lain Y · Akan datang Z" plus the `<x-material-status>` badge (Cukup / Kurang n · sedang datang / Kurang n) for the quantity being typed. **Reserved** = what other **confirmed / partially delivered** SOs still have to deliver (drafts reserve nothing; the order being edited is left out). One rule in one place: new **`App\Support\SalesAvailability`** — `reserved($productIds, $exceptSalesOrderId)` and `check($needed, $stock, $reserved, $incoming)` → available = stock − reserved (≥0); **reservations beyond stock also eat incoming** (incoming free = incoming − (reserved − stock)); status enough / incoming / short. The SO detail page now uses the same class (its old inline query removed) — which also fixes a gap there: before, incoming was counted as fully free even when other orders had already reserved more than the stock. Verified (rolled back): stock 100, another confirmed SO for 30, new SO 90 → reserved 30, "Kurang 20"; editing the reserving SO itself shows reserved 0; detail page agrees; rule cases (20 stock / 30 reserved / 100 incoming → 90 free). Key "Reserved" = "Dipesan pelanggan lain".
- **SO form stock row redesigned + related documents (user, 2026-10-13, /ui-ux-pro-max).** The grey hint line is now three tiles — **Stok** (neutral), **Dipesan pelanggan lain** (amber when > 0), **Akan datang** (blue when > 0); zero tiles stay muted and are disabled buttons — plus the `<x-material-status>` badge. Reserved/incoming tiles are buttons (≥44 px tall, focus ring, aria-label with the number and "lihat pesanan / lihat dokumen") that call `showSources($productId)` and open one modal `stock-sources`: three summary stats, then "Dipesan pelanggan lain" (SOs: number, customer, still to deliver, deliver-by date, late in red) and "Akan datang" (POs / WOs / finished-awaiting-warehouse). **New shared component `<x-stock-source-list :rows :unit :empty>`** (icon per source, quantity, date/late) — also used by the SO detail's incoming modal now. Each document number links to its page **only if the user can `view` it** (Sales sees SO links; WO/PO numbers stay plain text for Sales; Manager sees all) and **opens in a new tab** so an unsaved SO draft isn't lost. Data: `IncomingStock` rows now carry `document` (the PO / WO model); new `SalesAvailability::reservedOrders($productIds, $except)`. **Mobile fix:** product rows were a 3-column grid on phones (qty / price / subtotal a third each, so "Rp" + price was cramped); now qty and price share the row half-and-half, subtotal gets its own line (label left, amount right, dashed divider), trash stays top-right. 8 keys (768). Verified (rolled back): confirmed SO 30 + released WO 100 → tiles show 30 / 100; modal lists both; link rules per role; the reserving SO doesn't list itself; SO detail modal still works. Visual layout not verified — check desktop and phone width.
## Session 22 (2026-10-14 → 2026-10-15) — WIP/FG always in Pcs, PO PDF for suppliers, tax decisions + estimated PPN, product highlighted on Hasil Produksi, overhead (BOP) explained + rate breakdown per category, product cost for Manager and Sales

Same branch `main`, nothing committed this session. Assistant wrote all code (2026-10-05 rule), no code comments (2026-10-13 rule).

- **WIP and finished goods are always in Pcs (user, 2026-10-14).** Rule in one place: `ProductType::fixedUnit()` (Pcs for WIP/FG, null for raw). `SaveProductAction` overrides `unit_of_measure` with it, so nothing can save a WIP/FG in another unit. Products form: choosing WIP / Barang Jadi fills Satuan with Pcs and disables it, with the hint "WIP dan barang jadi selalu dihitung dalam Pcs."; switching back to raw clears it (new products) and Satuan is required again (`Rule::requiredIf`); placeholder now "mis. Kg, Liter". Data fix: migration `2026_10_14_070000_wip_and_finished_goods_in_pcs.php` set **Handle (WIP-003) Kg → Pcs** (it had no stock or documents); it refuses to run if a non-Pcs WIP/FG already has stock or movements (relabelling a counted quantity would change its meaning). Verified (rolled back): field behaviour, new FG saved in Pcs, the action given Kg for a WIP stores Pcs, raw still requires a unit. Formula form shows Pcs for Handle now.

- **PO PDF export for suppliers (user, 2026-10-14).** Decisions: **real PDF download** via **`barryvdh/laravel-dompdf` ^3.1** (new Composer dependency; dompdf 3.1.6); exportable from **Approved onward** (Approved, Partially Received, Fully Received, Closed — not Draft/Pending/Rejected/Cancelled); letterhead = **company name only for now, logo later**. New `config/company.php` (`name` = env `COMPANY_NAME`, default **"PT SAI"** — not `APP_NAME`, which is the system name "SIM PT SAI"; `logo` = env `COMPANY_LOGO`, a path under `public/`, embedded as a base64 data URI when the file exists — **to add the logo later: put the file in `public/` and set `COMPANY_LOGO=images/logo.png` in `.env`**). `PurchaseOrderPolicy::export()` (`purchasing.view` + status). `App\Http\Controllers\Purchasing\DownloadPurchaseOrderPdfController` → route `purchasing.orders.pdf` (`GET /purchasing/orders/{purchaseOrder}/pdf`), A4, **font subsetting on** (1.1 MB → 29 KB), file name `PO-2026-000015.pdf`. View `resources/views/purchasing/orders/pdf.blade.php` — tables + inline CSS only (dompdf doesn't do Tailwind/flexbox), DejaVu Sans: header (company / PESANAN PEMBELIAN + number), Kepada box (supplier, U.p. contact, address, phone · email), date box (Tanggal Pesanan, Perkiraan Pengiriman, Kode Pemasok), items table (No, Kode, Barang, Jumlah + unit, Harga Satuan, Subtotal), Total, note (state PO number on delivery note/invoice; prices exclude tax), signatures Dibuat oleh / Disetujui oleh + approval date, footer with generation time. PO detail header: **"Unduh PDF"** button (plain link, no `wire:navigate`) shown only when `export` allows. 13 keys (783). Verified: policy per status × role, 200 `application/pdf` attachment, PDF content checked visually, button shown on Approved / hidden on Draft.

- **Tax decisions (user, 2026-10-14) + estimated PPN on SO/PO.** Explained: tax is calculated on the **invoice (faktur)**, not on orders (orders change / deliver partially / cancel; faktur pajak goes with the invoice). Sales invoice → **PPN Keluaran** (liability): Dr Piutang / Cr Penjualan / Cr PPN Keluaran. Purchase invoice → **PPN Masukan** (asset, credited against Keluaran): Dr Barang Diterima Belum Difakturkan / Dr PPN Masukan / Cr Utang Usaha. **PPN Masukan is not inventory cost** — FIFO lots, issues, WO costs, COGS unaffected. Rate note: 12% on a tax base of 11/12 for ordinary goods = effectively 11%. **Decisions:** (1) **PT SAI is a PKP** — sales invoices carry PPN Keluaran, PPN Masukan is credited; (2) **tax rate typed per invoice** (ERD: `tax_rate decimal(5,2)` added to `sales_invoices` and `purchase_invoices`; purchase side copies the supplier's faktur pajak); (3) **SO and PO show an estimated PPN, informational only**. Built for (3): `config/tax.php` (`estimate_rate`, env `TAX_ESTIMATE_RATE`, default 11 — estimate only, never stored or journaled), `App\Support\TaxEstimate::for($amount)` → rate / tax / total (bcmath), component `<x-tax-estimate :amount>` = two small lines inside the accent Total card ("Perkiraan PPN 11% +Rp …", "Perkiraan termasuk PPN Rp …", title + sr-only "Hanya perkiraan — pajak sebenarnya ditetapkan di faktur."). The Total itself stays tax-exclusive (the order's real value). Used on SO form, SO detail, PO create, PO detail. **Not on the PO PDF** (the supplier's own faktur sets PPN). 3 keys (786). Verified: PO-2026-000015 4.400.000 → +484.000 / 4.884.000; SO form 30 × 45.000 → +148.500 / 1.498.500.

- **Hasil Produksi screens highlight the product made (user, 2026-10-14).** New shared component **`<x-produced-product :work-order>`**: header card (accent left border, cube icon, "Produk yang dihasilkan", product name large + type badge + code; Perintah Kerja link / Pusat Kerja / Target on the right, below on phones). Used on the **form** (create/edit) and the **detail** page, replacing the small grey "Untuk WO-… · product · …" subtitle. **List:** "Menunggu hasil" tab rows lead with the product name (semibold) + type badge, code · WO number below; "Hasil produksi" tab has a new first column **Produk yang dihasilkan** (name + badge, code) and WO is its own plain column (colspan 7 → 8). Verified: detail, both list tabs, create form render the product with its badge (create tested in a rolled-back transaction). Visual layout not verified.

- **Overhead (BOP) categories decided (user, 2026-10-15).** Explained: the work center rate is not a separate cost — it is *all* overhead combined into one number per machine hour: rate = estimated monthly overhead for that work center ÷ budgeted machine hours (example: Molding 30 jt electricity share + 18 jt machine depreciation + 4 jt maintenance + 3 jt other = 55 jt ÷ 1.800 h ≈ Rp 30.556/h). The app currently stores only the final rate; the breakdown lives in Akuntansi's own spreadsheet. **Decision:** overhead categories = **electricity, machine depreciation, mold depreciation, maintenance, other** (user added mold depreciation and maintenance; indirect labor, indirect materials and building costs were offered and not chosen — they fall under "other" if they occur). ERD: new `Enum overhead_category` (electricity, machine_depreciation, mold_depreciation, maintenance, other); `overhead_expenses.category` now uses it (was free text 'electricity | depreciation | other'). Nothing built uses it yet (`overhead_expenses` isn't built). **Then decided: B — store the calculation** (see next entry).
- **Overhead rate stores its calculation (user, 2026-10-15: "B, store the calculation").** Explained first: a **machine hour** = one machine running one hour — a measure of activity (hours), not a cost; electricity is a cost (rupiah) that grows with machine hours, so machine hours are used to share it (and depreciation, mold wear, maintenance, which also grow with run time) among WOs = the **cost driver**; labor hours would overcharge the assembly line ×4, pieces would ignore that a body takes longer than a lid. **Built:** enum **`App\Enums\OverheadCategory`** (electricity, machine_depreciation, mold_depreciation, maintenance, other; `label()`, `hint()`) — to be reused by `overhead_expenses`. Migration `production/2026_10_15_100000_add_breakdown_to_overhead_rates.php` (**run with `--path`**): `overhead_rates.budgeted_machine_hours` (nullable — null = old rate without breakdown) + new table **`overhead_rate_components`** (overhead_rate_id cascade, category, amount; unique rate+category). Model `OverheadRateComponent`; `OverheadRate::components()`, `hasBreakdown()`, `budgetedTotal()`. **`SetOverheadRateAction::handle($workCenter, array $components, $budgetedHours, $effectiveFrom, $createdBy)`** — validates (no negative amounts, total > 0, hours > 0 → `InvalidOverheadEstimateException` NEGATIVE_AMOUNT / NO_COSTS / NO_HOURS), computes **`rate_per_hour = rateFor(total, hours)`** (bcmath, half-up to 2 decimals; the same static method feeds the dialog's live preview), stores only non-zero components; effective-dating unchanged. `ProductionSeeder` now seeds breakdowns that reproduce the old rates (Molding 27 jt ÷ 1.800 h = 15.000; Assembly 8 jt ÷ 200 h = 40.000). **Page `/accounting/overhead-rates`:** each card shows the rate, "Rp total ÷ n jam mesin per bulan" and the five categories with amount, % share and a thin bar (empty ones muted); old rates show "Diisi sebagai satu angka, sebelum tarif memiliki rincian…". Set Rate dialog: five Rp inputs with hints, budgeted machine hours (hint 9 × 8 × 25 = 1.800), live accent preview (rate + "total ÷ hours", `aria-live`), pre-filled from the current rate when it has a breakdown. History lists each rate's components and "total ÷ hours" (old ones "tanpa rincian"). WOs still copy only `rate_per_hour` (nothing downstream changed). 22 keys (808). ERD: `budgeted_machine_hours` + `overhead_rate_components`. **Verified (rolled back):** 55 jt ÷ 1.800 → 30.555,56 (shown Rp 30.556) both in preview and stored; previous rate closed the day before; 0 hours / no costs / negative refused; card + history + pre-fill; WO create copies 30.555,56; Production forbidden. Part of Next Step #34 (`budgeted_amount/budgeted_hours`) is now done.
- **Electricity split + kWh (user, 2026-10-15).** Explained with example data (illustrative, not seeded): PLN Rp 1.115/kWh; molding machine ≈12 kW × 1.800 h, assembly line ≈6 kW × 200 h, general 3.000 kWh → bill Rp 28.767.000. Three ways to split one meter between work centers for the **rate estimate**: A by machine hours (90/10 — overcharges low-power assembly ≈ Rp 1,2 jt), **B by estimated kWh (kW × hours) + general part by machine hours** (Molding 27.094.500 / Assembly 1.672.500), C sub-meters (not available). The split only affects the estimate; actuals and the variance stay factory-wide, so split errors shift cost between work centers but never lose money. Full example rates: Molding 68.469.500 ÷ 1.800 = **Rp 38.039/h** (machine depr. 9 × (450 jt − 10%) ÷ 120 = 30.375.000; molds 330 jt ÷ 60 = 5.500.000; maintenance 4 jt; other 1,5 jt), Assembly 4.922.500 ÷ 200 = **Rp 24.613/h**. **Decision: A — kWh stays outside the app** (in Akuntansi's worksheet); the app stores only the rupiah amount per category. Not chosen (can revisit): storing kW/tariff to compute the electricity line in the rate dialog; recording actual kWh on `overhead_expenses` to split the electricity variance into usage vs. tariff.
- **Overhead explained end to end + variance to HPP (user, 2026-10-15).** Taught: the three cost elements (bahan baku and tenaga kerja traceable per WO; overhead not), why overhead must be in unit cost, why it is estimated in advance (the bill arrives after the month), the "machine rental per hour" picture, the cycle rate → applied on each WO (6 h × 38.039 = 228.234 in a 300-body WO, unit Rp 5.341) → actual bills → under/over-applied (example applied 71.244.720 vs actual 77.125.000 = 5.880.280 under-applied; causes: fewer hours than budget for fixed depreciation, electricity price, extra maintenance). Actual costs: a separate Akuntansi menu **Biaya Overhead Aktual** (`overhead_expenses`, factory-wide, same five categories, several rows per category allowed), entered **any time while the period is open** — entry date ≠ `period` (October's PLN bill entered 5 Nov with period October); no future periods; locked after closing (still to confirm); mid-month comparisons look low (depreciation / electricity arrive late) — display options A list only / B estimate vs actual with "belum masuk" markers, undecided. A product produced and sold in October carries the *estimated* overhead; documents and lots are never re-costed — the closing journal corrects the total. **Decision: Option 1 — the whole variance goes to HPP of that month** (not prorated to inventory; also what PSAK 14 / IAS 2 expect for under-absorption from low volume). ERD note on `overhead_period_closings.variance`. Profit for a month is only final after its overhead closing.
- **Product cost shown to Manager and Sales (user, 2026-10-15).** (1) Products page: Manager sees **average cost** to set the default selling price; (2) SO form: Sales sees **which lots the sale would come from** and the cost, so they don't undersell. **Access change: `costs.view` now = Akuntansi, Manager, Sales** (was Akuntansi, Manager — Session 19 rule). Sales can't open warehouse/production pages, so in practice this only reveals costs on sales pages; Warehouse and Production still see none. **One rule, one place:** new scope **`InventoryLot::availableFifo($productId)`** (qty > 0, `received_at`, `id`) — `DrawStockFifoAction::lockAvailableLots()` now uses it (verified same lots, same order) and so does the preview. New **`App\Support\ProductCost`**: `forProducts($ids)` → `average` = Σ `value_remaining` ÷ Σ `quantity_remaining` of lots in stock, `stock`, `last` (latest lot's unit cost, used when stock is 0); `fifoPreview($productId, $qty, $skip)` → lots oldest-first **after skipping `$skip` = quantity already reserved by other confirmed SOs** (they deliver first), with `covered`, `uncovered`, `average`; `margin($price, $cost)` = (price − cost) ÷ price, 1 decimal. **Products page:** new column **Biaya rata-rata** (or the last lot's cost with "lot terakhir, stok habis"), selling price shows its margin below (red when negative); edit modal (FG) shows "Biaya rata-rata stok (n Pcs) Rp …" + live "Margin pada harga ini" (red "di bawah biaya"); selling price input now `wire:model.live`. Products with no lots: "Belum ada biaya — produk ini belum pernah diproduksi." **SO form (each row, `@can('costs.view')`):** cost line "Biaya ≈ Rp …/Pcs · margin …%" (whole box red + "Di bawah biaya" when the price is below cost; amber note when part of the quantity has no free stock, "cost not known yet"; quantity empty → cost of the next unit); "Lihat lot" opens the stock modal, which now starts with **Lot yang akan dipakai pesanan ini** (lot, received date, qty, cost/unit, value; footer average + margin; note that it's an estimate — the warehouse takes the actual lots at delivery). 18 keys (826). **Verified (rolled back):** gate per role; averages for all products; FG-001 Rp 17.404 → 61,3% at 45.000, red at 1.000 / 5.000; lot table LOT-2026-000023 30 × 17.404; after another SO confirmed the whole lot, a new order of 10 shows no free lot / 10 uncovered. Not done: cost on the SO **detail** page.
- **Work center = all its machines; no mold depreciation for the assembly line (user, 2026-10-15).** Explained: Molding = all 9 machines; rate = total overhead of the 9 ÷ total hours of the 9 = cost of one machine running one hour (200 h × 38.039 = 1/9 of the total); a WO is charged its own machine hours regardless of which machine; one shared rate is valid because the 9 machines are identical — machines of different size would need separate work centers (e.g. Molding Kecil / Besar). **Built:** migration `production/2026_10_15_110000_add_uses_molds_to_work_centers.php` (run with `--path`): `work_centers.uses_molds` (MOLDING true, ASSEMBLY false; refuses to run if a no-mold work center already has a mold component). `WorkCenter::overheadCategories()` = the categories that apply (drops `mold_depreciation` when `uses_molds` is false). `SetOverheadRateAction` refuses a mold amount for such a work center (`InvalidOverheadEstimateException::CATEGORY_NOT_USED` → "Penyusutan cetakan tidak berlaku untuk pusat kerja ini."); the Set Rate dialog, card breakdown and amounts only use the applicable categories. Seeder: Assembly without a mold key. ERD: `uses_molds`. Verified (rolled back): Assembly dialog 4 fields / Molding 5; action refuses mold for Assembly; Assembly 4.922.500 ÷ 200 = 24.612,50 saved, card has no mold line.
- **Questions answered (teaching, no code):**
  - **Product first, then formula.** `production_formulas.product_id` points at `products`, and every material row points at a product too, so the order is bottom-up: raw materials → WIP product → WIP formula → FG product → FG formula. It also matches the roles: Manajemen decides *what* is made/sold ("Mengelola Data Produk"), Produksi decides *how* ("Mengelola Formula Produksi"). A product can exist without a formula (raw materials never have one); a formula cannot exist without its product.
  - **How many Pengeluaran Bahan per WO: as many as needed** (decided 2026-10-08: partial issues). Guarded by: only while the WO is Released / In Progress; the form pre-fills plan − already issued; stock only leaves on Post; the first posted issue moves the WO to In Progress; all issues add up on the WO detail and in the production result.
  - **When tax is calculated** — on the invoice, see the tax entry above.

## Session 23 (2026-10-18) — PO gets a "Biaya Lainnya" tab, mirroring SO's other-charges feature

Branch `main`, picked up right after master data was committed and pushed (commit `6914b9e`). Assistant wrote all code (2026-10-05 rule), no code comments (2026-10-13 rule). This is the first slice of Next Steps' "PO with the same tab layout as the SO" item — scoped to just the charges feature, not a full redesign of the PO pages.

- **New table `purchase_order_charges`** (migration `2026_10_18_000000_create_purchase_order_charges_table.php`, root folder, no `--path` needed): `purchase_order_id` (cascade), `description`, `amount` unsigned decimal(14,2) — exact mirror of `sales_order_charges` (no timestamps). Model `PurchaseOrderCharge` mirrors `SalesOrderCharge` (`#[Guarded(['id'])]`, `amount` cast `decimal:2`, `belongsTo(PurchaseOrder)`).
- **`PurchaseOrder` model:** new `charges(): HasMany` + `chargesTotal()` (same loaded-collection reduce as `SalesOrder::chargesTotal()`); `recalculateTotal()` now sums `items` subtotal **and** `charges` amount via a fresh query (`$this->charges()->sum('amount')`), not the loaded collection — same reasoning as the existing items sum, so it self-heals regardless of what's currently loaded in memory.
- **Decided (user, asked): charges also appear on the supplier PDF** — not kept internal-only. `DownloadPurchaseOrderPdfController` now eager-loads `charges`; the PDF (`purchasing/orders/pdf.blade.php`) only grows a Subtotal + per-charge row + Total breakdown **when charges exist** — unchanged appearance for every PO without one, matching the "don't add complexity that isn't needed" rule.
- **Create PO page** (`⚡create.blade.php`): kept the existing Step 1/Step 2 wizard, but Step 2's header is now an `<x-tab-bar>` (reused from SO, no changes to the component) switching between **Line Items** and **Other charges** (translated "Biaya Lainnya" — reused SO's existing `lang/id.json` keys, no new UI strings needed). New `charges` array + `activeTab` property, `addChargeRow()`/`removeChargeRow()`, a charges table (desc + Rp amount + remove) styled like SO's own charges tab. `estimatedTotal()` now folds in a new `chargesTotal()` computed; Order Summary card shows a "Biaya Lainnya" row only when non-zero. On save, charges are filtered (blank rows dropped) and validated (`required|string|max:255` / `required|numeric|gt:0`) the same way items are; a validation failure switches `activeTab` to whichever tab actually has errors (mirrors SO's `save()`).
- **View PO page** (`⚡show.blade.php`): same tab bar now sits above the Line Items table (replacing the old bare `<h2>Line Items</h2>`), switching to a new **Other charges** tab — a table (desktop) + cards (mobile) with inline per-row edit/remove and an "add new charge" row at the bottom, built to match the *existing* item-editing convention on this page (granular row actions, not SO's whole-form resave) rather than copying SO's exact markup. Three new granular Actions, mirroring `Add/Update/RemovePurchaseOrderItemAction` exactly: **`AddPurchaseOrderChargeAction`**, **`UpdatePurchaseOrderChargeAction`**, **`RemovePurchaseOrderChargeAction`** — all call `$po->ensureIsEditable()` first (Draft-only, same as items) and `$po->recalculateTotal()` after. Order Summary card gains an "Other charges" row when any exist.
- **`CreatePurchaseOrderData` DTO** gained an optional `charges` array (`list<array{description, amount}>`); `CreatePurchaseOrderAction` creates them inside the same transaction as items, then calls `recalculateTotal()` once at the end (unchanged position).
- **New translation keys** (3): `Charge added.` / `Charge updated.` / `Charge removed.` — every other string reused SO's existing keys (`Other charges`, `Add charge`, `Description`, `Amount`, `No other charges.`, etc.), so no new Indonesian copy needed for the UI itself.
- **ERD updated:** `purchase_order_charges` table added (mirrors `sales_order_charges`), added to the Pembelian TableGroup; `purchase_orders.total_amount` note updated to mention charges.
- **phpstan caught one real bug during review, fixed:** `AddPurchaseOrderChargeAction` originally created the charge via `$po->charges()->create([...])`, which phpstan flagged as returning `Model` instead of `PurchaseOrderCharge` (the relation's missing generics make `->create()` untyped) — switched to `new PurchaseOrderCharge([...]); $charge->save();`, the same explicit-construct pattern `AddPurchaseOrderItemAction` already uses, which doesn't have this problem. `CreatePurchaseOrderAction`'s charge-creation loop was changed the same way for consistency. Confirmed clean: `phpstan analyse` reports 0 errors on every new/changed file in this feature (the pre-existing codebase-wide relation-generics gap — Next Steps #17/#18, already deferred — still shows up on `PurchaseOrder`/`PurchaseOrderCharge`/the item Actions exactly as it already does on `SalesOrder` and the item Actions; not something this session introduced or fixed).
- **Verified (rolled back, via tinker):** create with 1 item (10 × 1.000 = 10.000) + 1 charge (50.000) → total 60.000; `chargesTotal()` → 50.000; add a second charge (20.000) → total 80.000; update that charge to 30.000 → total 90.000; remove it → total back to 60.000. All three Blade files (`⚡create`, `⚡show`, `pdf`) compile cleanly via `Blade::compileString()`. **Not verified:** actual browser rendering/interaction (tab switching, row add/remove UX, PDF visual output) — ask the user to check before relying on this in production.
- **Not done, flagged for later:** the broader "PO with the same tab layout as the SO" Next Step is still open beyond this — e.g. Create PO's Step 1/Step 2 wizard shape itself wasn't touched, only Step 2 grew a tab bar.
- **✅ RESOLVED (user, 2026-10-18): `overhead_expenses.work_center_id` question from Next Steps #34 — stays factory-wide, no work center column.** Grew out of a teaching discussion: the user asked whether a company-wide electricity bill should be split per work center when recording the *actual* cost too, the same way it already has to be split when setting the *budgeted rate* (Session 15). Explained the distinction first — **only genuinely shared cost sources need splitting at all** (one PLN meter for the whole factory = electricity); costs already tied to one work center's own assets (that work center's own machine/mold depreciation) were never shared in the first place, so there's nothing to split for those. Then the actual decision: keep `overhead_expenses` factory-wide only (no `work_center_id`), matching the existing Session 22 decision that the applied-vs-actual variance closes to HPP as **one** factory-wide number (`overhead_period_closings.variance`), not broken out per work center. **Why:** splitting the actual bill wouldn't make the number more true — it's still an estimate (by kWh, by hours, whatever method), just applied after the fact instead of before, and a per-work-center split would need a per-work-center closing/journal that hasn't been designed and isn't needed for what the business actually wants (one correct P&L number per month). **How to apply:** `overhead_expenses` stays as already designed in the ERD (period, category, amount, no work center); no schema change needed. The tradeoff — losing the ability to audit "was Molding's rate specifically wrong, or Assembly's" — is accepted; only the aggregate under/over-applied signal matters for now.

## Session 24 (2026-10-18) — Overhead variance journal taught; COA ("Mengelola Akun Perusahaan") built — Next Steps #35 done

Same day as Session 23, same branch `main`, uncommitted. Assistant wrote all code (2026-10-05 rule), no code comments (2026-10-13 rule).

**Teaching, no code (precedes the build below):**
- **Electricity split method, worked numerically** (user said the earlier kWh explanation didn't land — re-taught with a speed/distance analogy: kW is like km/h, kWh is like km, so power × time = energy, same as speed × time = distance). Re-derived the Session 15 example end to end: Molding 12 kW × 1.800 h = 21.600 kWh own use; general 3.000 kWh split by machine hours (90/10) → Molding +2.700 kWh; (21.600+2.700) × Rp 1.115/kWh = Rp 27.094.500 — matches the figure already on record from Session 15, confirming the method was represented correctly.
- **Where the split number goes:** confirmed it never enters the app as kW/kWh/tariff — only the final rupiah lands in one category field (Electricity) in the Set Rate dialog, gets summed with the other four categories, divided by budgeted hours → `rate_per_hour`, which is what every WO's machine hours get multiplied by afterward.
- **"Old HPP" question — does a product sold before the real bill arrives carry a wrong cost forever?** Yes, and that's intentional: a WO's overhead is baked into its lot's unit cost the moment the Production Result posts (e.g. 6 h × Rp 38.039 = Rp 228.234 on a 300-unit batch, ≈ Rp 761/unit), and that unit cost is **never rewritten** once the real bill arrives next month — re-opening an already-invoiced/paid sale to tweak its cost isn't done. The mis-estimate doesn't vanish or get redistributed into other sales either; it becomes **one separate adjustment to that month's total**, via the overhead closing (Session 22's decision): applied total vs actual total → variance → one journal line added on top of (not instead of) each sale's already-recorded COGS. This is why a month's profit is only *final* after its closing, even though every individual sale's recorded cost stays exactly as first computed.
- **Variance journal entry, debit/credit explained directly** (standard cost-accounting mechanics, not a business judgment call, so taught straight): **under-applied** (actual > applied, we undercharged products) → `Dr Harga Pokok Penjualan (Selisih BOP)` / `Cr BOP Sesungguhnya` — debiting HPP increases the expense to true it up. **Over-applied** (opposite) flips it: `Dr BOP Dibebankan` / `Cr Harga Pokok Penjualan (Selisih BOP)` — crediting HPP lowers an expense that was overstated during the month. Flagged honestly: the specific account names/keys (`BOP Sesungguhnya`, `Selisih BOP`) aren't in a chart-of-accounts yet — which is exactly what led into the build below.

**Built: Chart of Accounts ("Mengelola Akun Perusahaan") — resolves Next Steps #35.** User's own requirements: dynamic (add/edit/**delete**, not just deactivate — a first for this codebase's master data, see note below) and categorized by the 5 standard Indonesian account types. Two decisions asked and answered before building (materially changed the schema):
- **Account code is typed by the user, not system-generated** — unlike every other master-data code in this app (`product_code`, `supplier_code`), a COA code intentionally encodes a numbering scheme (1000s=Harta, 5000s=Beban, etc.) that only the accountant should control. So `account_code` is plain `fillable` input with a `unique` DB constraint + a `Rule::unique(...)->ignore($editingId)` form check — no auto-generation logic at all.
- **Flat for now, no sub-accounts.** The ERD's original sketch had a self-referencing `parent_id` for hierarchy (e.g. Harta Lancar / Harta Tetap under Harta) — dropped from both the migration and the ERD note, since the user only asked for the 5 top-level types and a parent-child UI (picker, circular-reference guards, cascade-on-delete decisions) is real complexity not currently needed. Noted in the ERD comment that it's addable later without touching existing data.

**Schema:** migration `accounting/2026_10_18_120000_create_accounts_table.php` (**new `database/migrations/accounting/` subfolder** — first table of the Accounting module; like `production/`/`sales/`/`purchase/`/`warehouse/`, needs `--path=database/migrations/accounting` since nothing calls `loadMigrationsFrom()` for it, see Next Steps #22's still-open note about this project-wide gap): `account_code` unique, `account_name`, `type`, `normal_balance`, `is_active` default true — no `parent_id`, no timestamps gap (has them, unlike the SO/PO charge tables). New enums **`App\Enums\AccountType`** (asset/liability/equity/revenue/expense; `label()` → Harta/Kewajiban/Modal/Pendapatan/Beban; `badgeClasses()`) and **`App\Enums\NormalBalance`** (debit/credit; `label()` → Debit/Kredit).

**Normal balance is derived, not chosen — on the enum itself:** `AccountType::normalBalance()` is a `match` (Asset/Expense → Debit; Liability/Equity/Revenue → Credit) — standard double-entry bookkeeping fact, not a business judgment call, so no option was given to pick an inconsistent combination. On the model, `normal_balance` is `#[Guarded]` (same "system-derived fields are never mass-assignable" rule as `products.current_stock`/`purchase_orders.total_amount`) — `SaveAccountAction` sets it via direct property assignment (`$account->normal_balance = $type->normalBalance(); $account->save();`), same pattern as every other guarded-field write in this codebase.

**`SaveAccountAction`** (`app/Actions/MasterData/`, alongside `SaveProductAction`/`SaveSupplierAction`) — handles both create and update in one method, mirroring `SaveProductAction`'s shape exactly. **`DeleteAccountAction`** — a genuinely new precedent: every other master data in this app (Product, Supplier, Employee) is **deactivate-only** by deliberate earlier decision ("deactivate instead of delete for data referenced by history"), but the user explicitly asked for real delete here. Built as a one-line Action for now (`$account->delete()`) — safe today only because `journal_entries`/`journal_entry_lines` don't exist yet, so nothing can reference an account. **Flagged, not built:** once journals exist, `DeleteAccountAction` will need a guard (block delete if any `journal_entry_lines.account_id` references it, suggest deactivating instead) — same shape as `RemovePurchaseOrderItemAction` not needing an extra "zero receipts" check only because the state machine already guarantees it; here, nothing guarantees it yet, so this is a deliberate, temporary gap, not an oversight.

**Gate `accounts.manage`** (Accounting, Manager — same two roles as `labor-rates.manage`/`overhead.manage`) + thin **`AccountPolicy`** (`viewAny`/`create`/`update`/`delete`, auto-discovered by Laravel's Model→Policy naming convention, no explicit registration needed, same as `ProductPolicy`). **Page** `resources/views/pages/accounting/accounts/⚡list.blade.php` — single list+modal page (no separate show page, mirrors the Products page's shape, not Suppliers' list+show split, since accounts have no sub-resource like a catalog): type-pill filter (All + the 5 categories with counts), search, show-inactive toggle, table with Code/Account/Category badge/Normal balance/Status/edit+delete actions, one modal reused for create and edit showing a live "Normal balance: Debit/Kredit" preview as the category is picked. Delete uses `wire:confirm` — the **first use of this directive in the codebase** (every other delete so far, e.g. removing a draft PO item/charge, is a low-stakes row-in-a-draft and skips confirmation; a standalone COA delete is a different risk level, so a native browser confirm was added rather than reusing the no-confirm convention). Route `accounting.accounts.list`; sidebar item "Chart of Accounts" (`rectangle-stack` icon) added to the Akuntansi group, after Overhead Rates. 20 new translation keys.

**Verified (rolled back, via tinker):** created Asset/Expense/Liability accounts → normal_balance came out debit/debit/credit correctly; edited an account's category from Asset to Liability and confirmed normal_balance re-derived from debit to credit on save (not left stale from creation); deleted an account, count dropped by one; duplicate `account_code` rejected at the DB unique-constraint level. Confirmed `accounts.manage` gate: `accounting`/`manager` → yes, every other role → no. `phpstan` clean except the pre-existing codebase-wide `scopeActive`/generics gap (identical to `Supplier::scopeActive`, not new). Blade file compiles via `Blade::compileString()`. **Not verified:** actual browser rendering (modal, picker, confirm dialog, pill filters) — ask the user to check.

**Not done, intentionally, flagged for later:** (1) the delete-guard once journals exist (above); (2) `config/accounts.php` mapping config keys (`material_variance`, `cogs`, etc.) to real `account_id`s — this COA page lets Akuntansi *create* the accounts, but nothing yet reads from this table to post a real journal entry (that's `journal_entries`/`journal_entry_lines`, still unbuilt); (3) sub-account hierarchy (`parent_id`), deferred per the decision above.

**Addendum same session — opening balance added (user, 2026-10-18).** Decided: **one field, a single positive Rp amount, assumed to sit on whichever side the account's own `normal_balance` is** (not a separate Debit/Credit pair) — simpler form, matches how most small-business accounting software does it, at the cost of not being able to represent a contra account starting out on its "wrong" side (e.g. accumulated depreciation, which is an Asset-type account that normally carries a *credit* balance) — not a concern yet since contra accounts aren't modeled in this COA at all. **Built:** `accounts.opening_balance` decimal(18,2) unsigned default 0 — added by **editing the still-uncommitted `accounting/2026_10_18_120000_create_accounts_table.php` migration in place** (per the project's own rule: fine while uncommitted, roll back + re-migrate; would need a new migration once committed) rather than a second migration. Fillable (user-typed, not guarded like `normal_balance`). Form gained a Saldo Awal Rp field with a live hint naming which side it's recorded on; the list table gained an **Opening balance** column; and a new reconciliation strip under the table sums every *active* account's opening balance into **Debit** vs **Credit** totals (bucketed by each account's own `normal_balance`) and shows **Seimbang** (green) when they match or **Tidak seimbang sebesar Rp X** (red) when they don't — a free correctness check that falls out of the data already being there, not a separate feature. 7 new translation keys. **Verified (rolled back, via tinker):** Kas (Harta, 10.000.000) + Modal Pemilik (Modal, 10.000.000) → Debit 10.000.000 / Credit 10.000.000 / diff 0 (balanced); adding Bank (Harta, 5.000.000) on top → Debit 15.000.000 / Credit 10.000.000 / diff 5.000.000 (correctly flags out-of-balance). **Still just a number on the row** — it doesn't itself create any `journal_entries` row; once that table exists, the natural next step is generating the real `opening`-type journal entry from these values (and probably locking `opening_balance` from editing once that journal exists, same "don't silently rewrite something already posted" reasoning used everywhere else in this codebase).

## Session 25 (2026-10-18) — Jurnal Umum built: core ledger infra + real auto-posting for all 6 existing cost flows

Same day as Sessions 23–24, same branch `main`, uncommitted. Planned via `EnterPlanMode`/`ExitPlanMode` first (user explicitly asked for the full scope — core infra **and** retrofitting the six already-shipped flows, not just a manual-entry page; reversal = Accounting **and** Manager). Assistant wrote all code, no code comments. This is the single biggest piece of work this session — months of transactions finally post real double-entry journal lines.

**Part 1 — core infra.** Migration `accounting/2026_10_18_130000_create_journal_entries_tables.php`: `journal_entries` (`journal_number` unique `JRN-{year}-{id}`, same two-step placeholder pattern as `po_number`; `entry_date`, `description`, `entry_type`, nullable `source_type`/`source_id` morph, `status` default posted, nullable self-referencing `reversal_of_id`, `created_by`, `created_at` only — immutable ledger like `stock_movements`) + `journal_entry_lines` (`journal_entry_id`, `account_id`, `debit`/`credit` decimal(18,2) default 0). New enums `App\Enums\JournalEntryType` (auto/manual/adjustment/closing/opening) and `App\Enums\JournalStatus` (**posted/reversed only — no draft**, matching the ERD's own design: a journal entry posts the instant it's created, corrections are a reversing entry, never an edit). Models `JournalEntry` (`#[Guarded(['id','journal_number','status'])]`, `transitionTo()` only allows Posted→Reversed, `totalDebit()`) and `JournalEntryLine`. **`App\Actions\Accounting\PostJournalEntryAction`** — the one place every journal (manual or automatic) gets created: drops zero-amount lines, refuses < 2 lines, refuses a line with both debit and credit set, refuses if `bccomp(sum debit, sum credit, 2) !== 0` (new `InvalidJournalEntryException`, reused for the "account role unmapped" case too), then creates the entry + lines in one transaction. **`ReverseJournalEntryAction`** — flips the original to Reversed via `transitionTo()`, builds a new entry with every line's debit/credit swapped (type `Adjustment`, `reversal_of_id` = original), both inside one outer `DB::transaction()` so a failed post can't leave the original half-reversed.

**Part 2 — dynamic account-to-role mapping.** Since the COA is fully user-managed (Session 24), every auto-posting flow needs to ask "which real account is *the* raw-material inventory account" without knowing what code/name Akuntansi actually gave it. Added `accounts.system_role` (nullable, unique when set — edited the still-uncommitted accounts migration in place again, rolled back + re-migrated). New enum **`App\Enums\AccountSystemRole`** — exactly the **9 roles** the 6 retrofits below need (`InventoryRaw`, `InventoryWip`, `InventoryFinished`, `GoodsReceivedNotInvoiced`, `WagesPayable`, `OverheadApplied`, `MaterialVariance`, `ProductionLoss`, `Cogs`), each with a Bahasa label + a hint explaining what moves through it. **Deliberately left out** the invoice-only roles (`vat_in`, `accounts_payable`, `sales_revenue`, etc.) the ERD's own comment lists — those stay out until the invoice feature that actually needs them exists, so nothing sits unused. **`App\Support\AccountResolver::forRole()`** — the one lookup every retrofit calls; throws a clear, translated error naming the missing role if no account has it set, rather than silently picking the wrong account or crashing on a null. COA page gained: a "System role" picker in the account form (options exclude roles already taken by *another* account; a synthetic "None" option since Flux's `x-picker` has no native way to clear a selection — had to prepend it manually), a small role-tag next to the account name in the list when set, and a "`7 of 9 system roles mapped`" status strip (amber until complete) so Akuntansi can see what's missing before relying on auto-posting.

**Part 3 — retrofitted all 6 existing flows + a new opening-balance trigger.** Each gets exactly one `PostJournalEntryAction` call added inside its existing transaction, right after it already computes the cost fields — no other behavior changed:
1. **`ReceivePurchaseOrderAction`** — `Dr InventoryRaw` / `Cr GoodsReceivedNotInvoiced`, amount = the same value the new lot gets.
2. **`PostMaterialIssueAction`** — `Dr InventoryWip` / `Cr InventoryRaw`, amount = Σ all lines' `total_cost` (one entry per issue document).
3. **`PostProductionResultAction`** — `Dr InventoryWip` (labor+overhead) / `Cr WagesPayable` (labor) / `Cr OverheadApplied` (overhead). **Material is deliberately not journaled here** — it already moved into WIP via step 2; `ProductionResult.material_cost` (issued − *expected* leftover) stays a reporting figure only.
4. **`PostProductionReceiptAction`** — `Dr InventoryFinished` (this receipt's value) + `Dr ProductionLoss` (`shortage_value`, only if > 0) / `Cr InventoryWip` for both combined (always equals `result.total_cost` by construction).
5. **`PostMaterialReturnAction`** — the subtle one, reasoned through by tracing the GL math forward to step 4: `Dr InventoryRaw` / `Cr InventoryWip` for the **actual** returned value, *plus* a variance correction, because `ProductionReceipt` later credits WIP by the *whole* `result.total_cost` (which embeds the *estimated* leftover from step 3), not by `issued − actualReturned` — so any gap between the estimate and reality has to be moved out of WIP now, at return time, or WIP never nets to zero for that batch. `variance_value = leftoverValue(expected) − returnedValue`: shortfall (positive) → `Dr MaterialVariance` / `Cr InventoryWip`; surplus (negative) → the reverse.
6. **`PostSalesDeliveryAction`** — `Dr Cogs` / `Cr InventoryFinished`. No revenue line — confirmed there's no price/invoice data reachable from this action; revenue stays on the not-yet-built Sales Invoice.
7. **Opening balance** — new, not a retrofit: a "Post opening balance" button on the COA page (Accounting/Manager), builds one `entry_type=opening` journal from every *active* account's `opening_balance` (one line per account, Dr/Cr per its own `normal_balance`), `source_type`/`source_id` left **null** per the ERD's own note. Refused if the existing balance check shows a mismatch, and refused a second time once any `opening`-type entry already exists (one-shot action).

Morph map gained 4 new keys (`material_issue`, `production_result`, `material_return`, `sales_delivery` — document-level, distinct from the existing per-*line* keys `material_issue_item`/`material_return_item`/`sales_delivery_item` already used by `stock_movements`; `purchase_order_receipt`/`production_receipt` were already registered and reused as-is).

**Real bug caught by integration testing, not just reading the code:** `PostMaterialReturnAction` originally computed its journal totals from `$return->items` — but that relation had been `load()`ed *before* the function's existing delete-and-recreate loop (it replaces every item row on each post), so the cached collection still pointed at the deleted generation of rows, making every return post a bogus empty/zero journal (caught as "needs at least two lines" when test-driving a real shortfall scenario). Fixed by querying `$return->items()->get()` fresh after the loop instead of trusting the stale relation property — a reminder that `$model->relation` (the cached property) and `$model->relation()->get()` (a fresh query) are not interchangeable once the underlying rows have changed since the property was first touched.

**Part 4 — manual Jurnal Umum UI**, for anything Akuntansi needs to post by hand. Gate `journal.manage` (Accounting, Manager — same pair reused for reverse too) + `JournalEntryPolicy`. Three pages under `resources/views/pages/accounting/journal/` (list/create/show, mirroring the PO shape rather than COA's single-modal shape, since a journal entry is itself a multi-line document worth its own page): `⚡list` (search/type/status filters, debit total per row via `withSum`); `⚡create` (pick any account per row via `x-picker`, a Debit *and* a Credit input per row — fill exactly one, since an arbitrary transfer between two accounts has no implied direction the way an account's own opening balance did; live running totals; Save disabled until balanced; posts immediately, no draft step); `⚡show` (all lines, a link to the source document when auto-generated, a link both ways between an entry and its reversal, a **Reverse** button gated to `journal.manage`). Routes `accounting.journal.*` (create registered before the `{journalEntry}` wildcard); sidebar item "Jurnal Umum" (`book-open`) under Akuntansi. ~23 new translation keys (most document-type labels for the "generated from" line were already there from earlier sessions).

**Verified (rolled back, via tinker, real data — not synthetic):** mapped all 9 roles to test accounts; received the one real `approved` PO in the dev DB → correct 2-line balanced entry. Built a full Work Order (formula #2, Badan Termos) → released → issued its planned materials → posted a production result (90 good/10 reject, 8 machine hours, deliberately used only 90% of issued material) → posted a *partial* production receipt (85 of 90 good, to force a real `shortage_value`) → posted a material return with a deliberate **shortfall** (returned 50% of the expected leftover) and separately a **zero-variance** case (returned exactly the expected leftover, confirming no spurious variance lines appear) — every journal balanced, every line matched the design above exactly, and the shortfall case is what surfaced the stale-collection bug (fixed, re-verified). Delivered against the one real confirmed SO in the dev DB → correct COGS entry. Reversed a real auto-generated entry → original flips to Reversed, new Adjustment entry has swapped lines, a second reverse attempt is correctly refused. Confirmed `journal.manage` gate: `accounting`/`manager` → yes, every other role → no. `phpstan` clean on every new/changed file except the same four pre-existing, already-deferred categories (relation generics, bcmath numeric-string, guarded-FK `int<0,max>` precision, missing `@property` annotations — Next Steps #17/#18) — confirmed via `git diff` that every flagged line in the retrofitted files was either untouched pre-existing code or a new line hitting the *same* already-accepted category, not a new class of issue.

**Not done, intentionally, flagged for later:** the surplus (negative-variance) branch of the Material Return journal was verified by careful sign-derivation and by confirming its mirror-image (shortfall) and zero-variance branches both work correctly — not separately integration-tested, since constructing a real surplus scenario needs a return quantity exceeding what was ever actually drawn, which isn't reachable through the normal UI flow; low risk given the code is a straight sign-flip of the already-proven shortfall branch. Sales/Purchase Invoices and Payments still don't exist — `Cogs`/`InventoryFinished` is as far as this app's books go until invoices exist to recognize revenue and real AP/AR; the invoice-only account roles stay out of the enum until then.

**✅ RESOLVED 2026-10-19 → option (1), built — see Session 26.** ~~**OPEN QUESTION:** should `accounts.opening_balance` (and the "Post Opening Balance" button/reconciliation strip on the COA page, Session 24) be removed?** User's reasoning, talked through but not yet actioned: COA is supposed to be pure master data (code, name, category, normal balance, system role) — none of which changes because a transaction happened. `opening_balance` is a point-in-time financial fact, not a structural property, and it goes stale the instant any real journal posts against that account (it never updates, so it stops meaning "current balance" immediately — unlike `products.current_stock`, which *is* kept live). It's also now redundant: the Jurnal Umum manual-entry page (this session, Part 4) can already do the exact same job — pick accounts, type amounts, must balance — as a real, auditable, reversible journal entry, which is strictly better than a bare field. **Two options on the table, neither chosen yet:** (1) remove the field + button entirely, rely on a manual Jurnal Umum entry (`entry_type = Opening`) for setting up starting balances — the clean answer, also unlocks a real "current balance per account" column later (computed live from `journal_entry_lines`, which the stale field currently can't offer); (2) keep it as a one-time seed value, explicitly understood as "ignore after first use." **If (1) is chosen:** removing it is a schema rollback on the still-uncommitted `accounts` migration (edit in place, roll back + re-migrate, same as every other change to this table this session) plus deleting the opening-balance UI section from `⚡list.blade.php` and the `postOpeningBalance()` method; the manual Jurnal Umum create page would need a small addition to let the entry's type be set to `Opening` instead of always `Manual`.~~

**Teaching, no code, continuing the same thread:** walked through *why* posting one account's opening balance in isolation is fragile — a lone `Dr Kas 1.000.000` needs *some* credit, and without other real balances to net against, that credit becomes a guessed equity plug (Modal/Laba Ditahan) every time, rather than the one coherent opening trial balance a real cutover needs (every known asset/liability entered together, with only the true leftover difference going to equity). Also clarified the Jurnal Umum entry form precisely, since it came up as a design concern: **per line**, debit and credit are mutually exclusive (filling both would mean "debit and credit the same account," which cancels to nothing — not a real movement); **across the whole entry**, both sides are naturally required since the entry can never balance otherwise (an all-debit or all-credit entry has a non-zero total on one side and zero on the other, so the balance check alone guarantees both sides appear, no extra rule needed). The **Post Entry** button is disabled live, the entire time the running totals don't match — this is the actual correctness guarantee; `PostJournalEntryAction`'s own balance check is a defense-in-depth backstop behind it, not the primary gate.

## Session 26 (2026-10-19 → 2026-10-20) — `opening_balance` removed, Jurnal Umum as a real journal book, PO charges into inventory cost (landed cost), Kepala Produksi dashboard with charts, production reports (Biaya Produksi + Persediaan Gudang)

- **PO charges now go into inventory cost (Next Steps #44, option A — user chose all three recommended sub-options).** (1) **Basis = by value:** each item's share = capitalised charges × item subtotal ÷ Σ items subtotal (works across mixed units). (2) **Per-charge flag:** new `purchase_order_charges.include_in_cost` boolean default true (edited the still-uncommitted migration in place; on dev DB added via `Schema::table` to keep the 1 existing charge); unticked = stays out of inventory cost (to be expensed at invoice time). (3) **Per-unit add-on:** `PurchaseOrderItem::chargeShare()` + `landedUnitCost()` = unit_price + share ÷ quantity_ordered (scale 6); `ReceivePurchaseOrderAction` now creates the lot with `unit_cost` = landed cost rounded to 2 dp and `value_remaining` = qty × exact landed cost rounded to 2 dp (rounding via `bcadd(x, '0.005', 2)`, PHP 8.3 has no `bcround`; values are always positive). The receipt journal already uses the lot value, so Dr Persediaan / Cr Barang Diterima Belum Difakturkan includes the freight automatically, and so does FIFO → material issue → production cost → HPP. Accepted trade-off: over/under-receipt capitalises slightly more/less than the PO's charge total. Charges are Draft-only edits, so they're frozen before any receipt. Receipts posted before this change keep their old (charge-free) lot cost — no backfill. ## Session 27 (2026-10-21) — Fix #38 (one open Material Issue draft per WO) · Faktur Pembelian + Pembayaran Pembelian (purchase cycle complete) · journal-style post/payment dialogs

Branch `main`, after commit `ce84225`. User picked "#38 fix, then Purchase Invoice" and asked for the error to be explained first. Assistant wrote the code, no code comments.
- **The bug, as explained:** `SaveMaterialIssueDraftAction` never checked for an existing draft; only the list page's "Buka draf" hint steered users. Two Gudang users opening the same WO before either saved both got the form pre-filled with plan − issued (e.g. 45 kg), both saved, both posted (over-issue is allowed by design) → 90 kg recorded for 45 kg physically issued: stock + FIFO lots 45 kg too low (later issues wrongly blocked), WO material cost doubled → wrong HPP per unit → finished-goods lot → HPP on sale, journal Dr WIP / Cr Persediaan posted twice, and at Retur Bahan a fictitious 45 kg "leftover" → false shortfall in Selisih Bahan.
- **Fix:** inside the save transaction the action now **locks the WO row** (`WorkOrder::whereKey()->lockForUpdate()`), then refuses with new **`MaterialIssueDraftExistsException`** (carries the existing draft; message names its number) if another draft exists for that WO — editing the *same* draft is excluded from the check. The lock is the point: two simultaneous saves now queue, so the second one sees the first draft. **Stronger than Sales Delivery's** `checkDeliverable()`, which checks for an open draft *before* its transaction without a lock (same theoretical race — noted, not changed). Form (`warehouse/outbound/material-issues/⚡form`): opening "create" for a WO that already has a draft **redirects to that draft's edit page** with a warning toast (same pattern as Hasil Produksi create); the save catches the new exception and toasts it. 1 translation key.
- **Verified (rolled back, temporary Released WO):** draft A saved → second new draft refused with "…sudah punya draf yang terbuka (MI-2026-000026)…" → editing A still works → opening create redirects to A's edit → after A is cancelled a new draft is allowed. True concurrency (two DB connections at once) not simulated; the row lock is what guarantees it.
- **Next (user's choice): Purchase Invoice (Faktur Pembelian).**
- **Faktur Pembelian — decided 2026-10-21 (all four recommended options, matching the existing ERD design):** (1) **1 faktur = 1 receipt batch** (`purchase_order_receipt_batch_id` unique — PO ↔ penerimaan ↔ faktur 3-way match). (2) Price difference → **Selisih Harga Pembelian** account (new system role); the received value clears Barang Diterima Belum Difakturkan exactly, lots keep their received cost. (3) **PO Biaya Lainnya:** capitalised charges are already inside the receipt value (landed cost, Session 26), so their share is **pre-filled** on the faktur from that receipt; **unticked** charges (admin, materai) are typed as extra lines and go to an **expense** account. (4) **Pembayaran Pembelian built together**: partial payments allowed, pick the Kas/Bank account, journal Dr Utang Usaha / Cr Kas-Bank; faktur status Belum dibayar / Dibayar sebagian / Lunas. Already settled earlier: Akuntansi (+ Manager) records it, Draft → Post, tax rate typed per faktur (Session 22), PPN Masukan credited, never inventory cost. Proposed (not yet confirmed): due date typed on the faktur (suppliers have no payment terms in the app); optional Nomor Faktur Pajak.

- **Faktur Pembelian + Pembayaran Pembelian built (2026-10-21)** — as decided above; spec confirmed by the user ("yes build it").
  - **Schema** — migration `accounting/2026_10_21_100000_create_purchase_invoices_tables.php` (**`--path=database/migrations/accounting`**): `purchase_invoices` (`internal_number` PI-{year}-{id}, supplier `invoice_number`, optional `tax_invoice_number`, PO, receipt batch, supplier, invoice/due date, `goods_amount`, `capitalized_charges`, `other_charges`, `subtotal` (= DPP), `receipt_value`, `price_variance`, `tax_rate`, `tax_amount`, `total_amount`, `amount_paid`, `status`, notes, created/posted by+at; **no DB unique on the batch** — "one non-cancelled faktur per batch" is enforced in the actions so a cancelled faktur can be replaced), `purchase_invoice_items` (per receipt line: qty fixed, `unit_price` editable, `amount`, `received_value`), `purchase_invoice_charges` (description, expense `account_id`, amount), `purchase_payments` (PAY-{year}-{id}, date, amount, `method`, `cash_account_id`, `reference`, `status` posted/cancelled, cancelled by+at). ERD updated to match (+ `draft` in `invoice_status`, the two new tables, TableGroup).
  - **Enums/roles:** `PurchaseInvoiceStatus` (draft, unpaid, partially_paid, paid, cancelled; `isPosted()`, badges), `PaymentMethod` (cash, bank_transfer, giro); payments reuse `DocumentStatus`. **3 new `AccountSystemRole`s: `AccountsPayable` (Utang Usaha), `VatIn` (PPN Masukan), `PurchasePriceVariance` (Selisih Harga Pembelian)** — COA status strip now counts 12 roles; posting refuses with the existing "role not mapped" message until Akuntansi maps them.
  - **Received value** = `PurchaseOrderReceipt::receivedValue()` = the amount that receipt's own auto-journal posted to Barang Diterima Belum Difakturkan (exact, incl. landed-cost freight); fallback `quantity_initial × unit_cost` for receipts that have no journal. Lot `value_remaining` is NOT usable (it shrinks as material is consumed).
  - **Actions (`App\Actions\Purchasing`):** `SavePurchaseInvoiceDraftAction` (locks the batch row, refuses a 2nd live faktur, validates dates (≤ today, due ≥ invoice), price ≥ 0, extra charges must use an active **expense** account; static `lines()`, `totals()` (tax = DPP × rate, half-up), `defaultCapitalizedCharges()` = Σ(received − qty×PO price) — reused by the form for the live summary), `PostPurchaseInvoiceAction` (journal dated `invoice_date`: Dr GRNI receipt_value + Dr each expense line + Dr PPN Masukan + Dr/Cr Selisih Harga / Cr Utang Usaha total; source `purchase_invoice`), `CancelPurchaseInvoiceAction` (draft → cancelled; posted → only if no live payments, reverses its journal), `RecordPurchasePaymentAction` (locks invoice; amount > 0 and ≤ balance; date ≥ invoice date, ≤ today; cash account must be an active **asset** account, not an inventory or PPN role; journal Dr Utang Usaha / Cr Kas-Bank dated `payment_date`, source `purchase_payment`; `syncPaymentStatus()`), `CancelPurchasePaymentAction` (reverses its journal, recomputes paid + status). Exceptions `InvalidPurchaseInvoiceException`, `InvalidPurchasePaymentException`. DTO `PurchaseInvoiceData`. Gate **`purchasing.invoice`** (Accounting, Manager). Morph keys `purchase_invoice`, `purchase_payment`; journal detail page labels them as the source.
  - **Pages** (`accounting/purchase-invoices/`, sidebar Akuntansi → "Faktur Pembelian"): `⚡list` (tabs "Penerimaan belum difakturkan" — with "Catat faktur" / "Buka draf" — and "Faktur" with status filter; "Utang Belum Dibayar" box with overdue count), `⚡form` (create from a batch / edit draft; header fields, goods table with PO vs faktur price + "Berbeda dari PO", Biaya persediaan (pre-filled), Biaya Lainnya rows with expense-account picker, PPN %, live summary incl. "Nilai saat diterima" and "Selisih harga" explanation; opening create for a batch that already has a faktur redirects to it), `⚡show` (status + overdue badge, tiles total / sudah dibayar / sisa / tanggal, three-way-match line PO → RCV → faktur, items, charges, payments list with per-payment cancel, summary, linked journal entries, modals: Post (explains the journal), Cancel, Catat pembayaran (pre-fills remaining balance)). ~122 translation keys.
  - **Verified (rolled back):** action-level end-to-end — new PO 100 × 30.000 + 200.000 freight (ticked) → receive 40 → GRNI +1.280.000; faktur at 30.500 + admin 5.000 + PPN 11% → goods 1.220.000, biaya persediaan 80.000, **selisih +20.000**, PPN 143.550, total 1.448.550; journal balanced, **GRNI for that receipt back to exactly 0**; 2nd faktur refused; partial 700.000 → Dibayar sebagian; overpay and paying from an expense account refused; rest → Lunas, Utang Usaha 0; cancel invoice with payments refused; cancel payments → cancel invoice → journal reversed, GRNI back, receipt invoiceable again. Page-level via Livewire (list → form with live summary → save → redirect-to-draft → post → two payments → Lunas in list, receipt leaves pending tab; Purchasing role blocked). Headless screenshots of list/form/show; fixed: "Paid" tile showed the status word "Lunas" → own key "Sudah Dibayar".
