## Session 28 (2026-10-23) — Biaya Overhead Aktual + month-end BOP closing (Next Steps #41) — built with NO new tables

Branch `main`, still uncommitted (together with Session 27). User picked "Overhead Aktual + closing" and added the constraint **"keep in mind to not add more tables if possible"**. Assistant wrote all code, no code comments.
- **Decisions (user, AskUserQuestion — all recommended options):** (1) **journal-based design, no new tables** — the ERD's `overhead_expenses` / `overhead_period_closings` were **removed from the ERD** (now 48 tables) and replaced by a design note; (2) **closed months are locked** — a late bill goes into the next open month; (3) mid-month view = **estimate vs actual per category** with a "Belum masuk" marker.
- **Where the data lives instead:** category = an account, via **5 new `AccountSystemRole`s** `OverheadElectricity`, `OverheadMachineDepreciation`, `OverheadMoldDepreciation`, `OverheadMaintenance`, `OverheadOther` (labels BOP Listrik / Penyusutan Mesin / Penyusutan Molding / Pemeliharaan / Lain-lain; `OverheadCategory::systemRole()` maps them) — COA strip now counts **17 roles**. A bill = one journal entry, `source_type = 'overhead_expense'`, `source_id = YYYYMM`, `entry_type = manual`, dated the last day of the period (or today for the current month): Dr BOP [kategori] / Cr the chosen account (active Harta/Kewajiban — Kas/Bank, utang, Akumulasi Penyusutan; inventory, PPN Masukan, Barang Diterima Belum Difakturkan, BOP Dibebankan excluded). The closing = one entry `entry_type = closing`, `source_type = 'overhead_closing'`, `source_id = YYYYMM`, dated month end. These two `source_type`s have no model/morph-map entry — only used for filtering and the journal page's source label (verified nothing loads `->source`).
- **`App\Support\OverheadPeriod`** (per `Y-m`): `key()`, `isFinished()`, `isFuture()`, `closingEntry()/isClosed()`, `expenseDate()`, `expenses()`, `balance(role)` = Σ debit − credit on that role's account for journal lines dated in the month **excluding the closing entry**, `applied()` (= − balance of BOP Dibebankan, i.e. credits from production results by **journal date**), `budget()` (Σ `overhead_rate_components` of each work center's rate in force at month end), `categories()` (budget, actual, entry count), `actualTotal()`, `closingPlan()` → Dr BOP Dibebankan applied / Cr each BOP category actual / selisih (actual − applied) Dr (under-applied) or Cr (over-applied) **Harga Pokok Penjualan** (Session 22 decision). Signs handled both ways.
- **Actions (`App\Actions\Accounting`):** `RecordOverheadExpenseAction` (refuses future month, amount ≤ 0, wrong credit account, and — under a lock — a closed month with the "book it in the next open month" message; static `plan()` for the preview), `CancelOverheadExpenseAction` (only while the month is open; **reversal dated the same day as the original** so it stays in that month — `ReverseJournalEntryAction::handle()` gained an optional `$entryDate`, default unchanged = today), `CloseOverheadPeriodAction` (only a finished month, not already closed, something to close). `InvalidOverheadEntryException` (PERIOD_CLOSED, ALREADY_CLOSED, FUTURE_PERIOD, INVALID_AMOUNT, INVALID_CREDIT_ACCOUNT, NOT_AN_EXPENSE, ALREADY_CANCELLED, NOT_FINISHED, NOTHING_TO_CLOSE). Gate: existing `overhead.manage` (Accounting, Manager).
- **Shared helper `App\Support\JournalPreview::resolve($plan, $sides, $pendingLabel)`** — turns a role/account plan into `<x-journal-preview>` lines (account name + code, "belum dipetakan", pending line); the Faktur Pembelian page was refactored to use it too (no duplicate resolver).
- **Page `/accounting/overhead`** (`pages::accounting.overhead.index`, sidebar Akuntansi → "Biaya Overhead Aktual"): month navigator + status (Terbuka / Ditutup — link to the closing journal / Bulan mendatang; "Bisa ditutup mulai 1 Nov 2026" for the current month); tiles Anggaran · Dibebankan ke Produksi · Aktual · Under/Over-applied; per-category table (anggaran, aktual, aktual vs anggaran, "Belum masuk" when budgeted but nothing recorded); recorded bills list (journal link, cancel while open, cancelled ones struck through); **"Catat biaya overhead" dialog with live journal preview**; **"Tutup [bulan]" dialog with the closing journal preview** + under/over-applied explanation; unmapped roles → amber warning + button disabled. ~55 translation keys (incl. separate `Open period` → "Terbuka", since `Open` = "Buka").
- **Verified (rolled back):** September 2026 with 2.000.000 applied (simulated production journal) → bills PLN 1.200.000 (Cr Kas) + penyusutan mesin 900.000 (Cr Akumulasi Penyusutan) + a wrong 500.000 cancelled (reversal dated 30 Sep) → actual 2.100.000 → closing JRN: **Dr BOP Dibebankan 2.000.000 / Cr BOP Listrik 1.200.000 / Cr BOP Penyusutan Mesin 900.000 / Dr HPP 100.000** (under-applied), balanced; refusals: late bill into closed Sep, cancel in closed Sep, close twice, close the current month, future month, crediting an inventory account, closing an empty month; late bill then booked into October. Page-level via Livewire (record with live preview, cancel, close) + headless screenshots of the page and both dialogs. Faktur Pembelian page re-tested after the refactor (all 9 steps pass). Note: September shows Anggaran Rp 0 because the dev overhead rates only start in October.
- **Not done / to know:** closing doesn't force months to be closed in order; manual Jurnal Umum entries dated into a closed month that touch BOP accounts are not blocked (only this page's flows are); "overhead rate accuracy" report (roadmap Layer 3) can now be built from `OverheadPeriod` (applied vs actual per month).

## Session 29 (2026-10-23) — Bug fix: semi-finished goods booked to the wrong inventory accounts · journal confirmation before every posting

Same day as Session 28, still uncommitted. Assistant wrote all code, no code comments.

**1. Bug found while building the confirmations (fixed, user chose "fix first").** WIP components (Badan / Tutup / Handle) were booked inconsistently: Penerimaan Produksi always **Dr Persediaan Barang Jadi**, Pengeluaran Barang always **Cr Persediaan Bahan Baku**, Retur Bahan always **Dr Persediaan Bahan Baku** — regardless of product type. Total inventory was right, but Barang Jadi was overstated (components went in, only sales take out) and Bahan Baku understated (components taken out of an account they never entered; could go negative). **Fix (user decision: new role, not reuse WIP):** new `AccountSystemRole::InventorySemiFinished` (**Persediaan Barang Setengah Jadi**), new **`ProductType::inventoryRole()`** (raw → Bahan Baku, wip → Barang Setengah Jadi, finished → Barang Jadi), helper **`App\Support\InventoryAccounts::group()`**; the account is now chosen from the product type in **ReceivePurchaseOrderAction, PostMaterialIssueAction, PostMaterialReturnAction, PostProductionReceiptAction, PostSalesDeliveryAction**. `Persediaan Barang Dalam Proses` (`InventoryWip`) now only means "cost of work orders still in production". Verified with a full rolled-back chain (molding WO for Badan → issue raw → result → receipt → return → assembly WO takes Badan): Badan enters and leaves through Barang Setengah Jadi, raw through Bahan Baku, WIP nets to 0. **Dev data:** journals posted before this fix keep the old (wrong) accounts — dev only. COA now has **18 roles**.

**2. Journal confirmation before every posting (user: "any action that post a journal … display confirmation popup saying what is going to be posted").** Decisions (user): **Gudang sees the accounts but no amounts** (keeps the Session 19 "warehouse pages hide costs" rule — `<x-journal-preview :hide-amounts>` shows ✓ per side + note "Nilai dihitung otomatis dengan biaya FIFO…"; shown when the user lacks `costs.view`), Produksi/Akuntansi/Manager see amounts; **reversals and cancellations also show the reversing journal**.
- **No "dry run"** (post + rollback) on purpose: MySQL doesn't roll back auto-increment, so it would leave gaps in JRN- numbering. Instead every action got a pure **`preview()`** plus a shared **`journalPlan()`** builder used by both the preview and the real posting: `ReceivePurchaseOrderAction::preview(array qty)` (+ `receiptValue()`), `PostMaterialIssueAction::preview()`, `PostProductionResultAction::preview()` (+ shared `laborRate()`), `PostProductionReceiptAction::preview()` (+ `receivedValue()`), `PostMaterialReturnAction::preview()` (Retur's two WIP lines merged into one, same net), `PostSalesDeliveryAction::preview()`. FIFO: `DrawStockFifoAction` refactored so `draw()` and new no-write `estimate()` share one `slices()` calculation (+ `availableLots()` without lock). Reversals: `JournalPreview::reversalOf($entry)` (lines swapped).
- **UI:** new `<x-journal-confirm :plan :hide-amounts>` (resolves via `JournalPreview`, warns about unmapped roles, falls back to "Jurnal akan dihitung saat posting" if a preview can't be computed). Added to the Post dialogs of Pengeluaran Barang, Hasil Produksi, Penerimaan Produksi, Retur Bahan, Pengiriman Penjualan; **Item Receipt** got a new confirm step (`confirmSubmit()` runs the same checks, then the dialog's button submits); **Jurnal Umum** manual entry (`confirmPost()`) and **Balik jurnal** dialog (replaced `wire:confirm`); Faktur Pembelian cancel dialog shows the reversal; **cancel payment** and **cancel overhead entry** now open a dialog with the reversal (replaced `wire:confirm`). Already had previews: faktur post, payment, overhead bill, overhead close. 13 translation keys.
- **Verified (rolled back):** for every flow the **preview equals the posted journal exactly** (item receipt 2 lines; material issue FIFO; production result; production receipt with shortage → Kerugian Produksi; Retur with shortfall → Selisih Bahan; Badan issue across several lots); page-level: Gudang sees no Rp, Manager/Produksi see Rp, posting/submitting/reversing from each dialog works; screenshots of the Gudang and Manager dialogs. **Not tested with a real document: the Pengiriman Penjualan preview** (no sales deliveries exist in dev) — it uses the same `estimate()` + plan builder as Pengeluaran Barang, which matched.
- **⚠️ Dev COA naming (user's data, not changed):** account **1003 "Persediaan Barang Setengah Jadi" is mapped to the WIP role (Barang Dalam Proses)**, and **1006 "Persediaan Bahan Setengah Jadi"** to the new semi-finished role — the names are swapped/misleading; suggested renaming 1003 → "Persediaan Barang Dalam Proses" and 1006 → "Persediaan Barang Setengah Jadi". Still unmapped: BOP Penyusutan Mesin, BOP Penyusutan Molding, BOP Pemeliharaan, BOP Lain-lain.

## Session 30 (2026-10-23 → 10-25) — Permintaan Pembelian (Purchase Requisition) — ✅ DONE, standalone

Still uncommitted (together with Sessions 27–29). Assistant wrote all code, no code comments.

**Design change mid-build (user, 2026-10-25): "actually i want permintaan pembelian to stand on its own".** First version tied 1 request = 1 WO; replaced by:
- **No work order link at all** (`work_order_id` dropped from the migration before it was ever used; `WorkOrder::purchaseRequisitions()` and the WORK_ORDER_CLOSED exception reason removed).
- **No notes at all (user, 2026-10-25: "remove catatan per line item. and keterangan field")** — `purchase_requisitions.notes` and `purchase_requisition_items.note` removed from the migration (re-run as a single file while the tables were empty; ID counters reset to 1). A request is just materials + quantities + needed-by date; the decline reason is the only free text.
- **The form suggests what is short** and allows any other purchasable material freely.
- **Status label "Diproses" renamed "Dijadikan PO"** (user, 2026-10-26: it read as "still in progress"). Dijadikan PO / Ditolak / Dibatalkan are terminal for the request; delivery progress belongs to the PO.
- **Requests follow a rejected PO (user, 2026-10-26).** A rejected PO can be reopened (Rejected → Draft), so rejection is not final: `RejectPurchaseOrderAction` calls `ResolvePurchaseRequisitionAction::reopenForRejectedOrder()` → its Dijadikan PO requests go back to Diajukan but **keep `purchase_order_id`** (show page: amber "Terbuka kembali" banner with the PO's rejection reason; list: "PO … ditolak"). `ReopenPurchaseOrderAction` calls `restoreForReopenedOrder()` → requests still Diajukan and still pointing at that PO return to Dijadikan PO; ones taken by another PO, declined or cancelled meanwhile are left alone. No PO cancel action exists yet (`Cancelled` is in the enum but unused) — when one is built it should call `reopenForRejectedOrder()` too. Verified 7/7 (rolled back).
- Unchanged decisions: only Produksi (+Manager) creates; no approval; Purchasing turns one or more requests into one PO (same materials summed, saving marks them Diproses + links the PO); Purchasing may decline with a reason; Produksi may cancel while still open.

**Suggestion rule (`App\Support\MaterialShortage::suggestions()`)**, per active purchasable material:
- needed = Σ over WOs Draft/Released/In Progress of (planned − posted issued), never below 0 per WO line.
- covered = current stock + on order (open qty of POs Draft → Partially received, `openPurchaseOrderStatuses()`) + already requested (requests still Submitted).
- shortage = needed − covered; reorder gap = reorder_point − covered (only when reorder_point > 0); **suggested = the larger of the two**; rows with suggested > 0, biggest first, with the WO numbers that need it.

**Built:**
- Migration `purchase/2026_10_23_100000_create_purchase_requisitions_tables.php` — **run only this file** (`php artisan migrate --path=database/migrations/purchase/2026_10_23_100000_create_purchase_requisitions_tables.php`): the old `2026_09_12_111713_create_purchase_order_attachments` shows Pending in dev although its table exists (pre-existing mismatch — don't migrate the whole folder). Tables `purchase_requisitions` (PR-{year}-{id:06}, needed_by, status, rejection_reason, purchase_order_id nullOnDelete, created_by, processed_by/at) + `purchase_requisition_items` (product, quantity decimal(12,2); unique request+product).
- Enum `PurchaseRequisitionStatus` (Submitted/Processed/Rejected/Cancelled = Diajukan/Diproses/Ditolak/Dibatalkan); models `PurchaseRequisition` (`ensureIsSubmitted()`), `PurchaseRequisitionItem`; `PurchaseOrder::purchaseRequisitions()`; `InvalidPurchaseRequisitionException` (NOT_OPEN, NO_LINES, NOT_PURCHASABLE, NEEDED_BY_PAST, REASON_REQUIRED).

**Testing note:** `php artisan tinker <file>` must be run with `< /dev/null`, otherwise it can sit at the interactive prompt forever. `Livewire::test()` needs `Livewire::withQueryParams([...])` for query strings and reports a failed `Gate::authorize` as a 403 response (`assertForbidden()`), not an exception.

## Session 31 (2026-10-26) — Faktur Penjualan + Penerimaan Pembayaran — ✅ DONE

Uncommitted (with Sessions 27–30). Assistant wrote all code, no code comments.

**Decisions (user, 2026-10-26):** one invoice = one posted delivery (ERD); the SO's discount % applies to each invoice's goods, and the SO's extra charges (sales_order_charges) are billed once, on the order's **first posted** invoice; **Draft → Post** (same as Faktur Pembelian); **Akuntansi + Manager** issue invoices and record payments, Sales only views. Assistant defaults: due date pre-filled from the SO's payment terms (Cash = same day, NET n = +n days, editable); PPN typed per invoice, default 11; partial payments allowed; payment number `RCP-`.

**Calculation (`SaveSalesInvoiceDraftAction::totals()`):** goods = Σ delivered qty × SO unit price (lines are **not stored** — both are locked once the SO is confirmed, so no items table); discount = `SaveSalesOrderDraftAction::discountAmount(goods, SO %)`; charges = SO charges total unless another **posted** invoice of the SO already carries them; DPP (subtotal) = goods − discount + charges; PPN = DPP × rate (rounded half-up); total = DPP + PPN. **Totals are recalculated at posting** under a lock on the SO row, so two drafts that both show the charges can never both bill them; if a posted invoice with the charges is cancelled, the next invoice posted picks them up. The post dialog warns when the total changed since the draft was saved.

**Journals:** post = Dr Piutang Usaha (total) / Cr Penjualan (DPP) / Cr PPN Keluaran (PPN, omitted at 0%), dated invoice date; payment = Dr Kas/Bank (chosen active asset account, not inventory / PPN Masukan / Piutang) / Cr Piutang Usaha; cancel invoice (only without live payments) and cancel payment = reversing journal.

**Built:**
- Migration `accounting/2026_10_26_100000_create_sales_invoices_tables.php` (**run with `--path`** like the other accounting migrations): `sales_invoices` (SI-{year}-{id}, SO, delivery, customer, dates, goods/discount %/discount/charges/subtotal/tax rate/tax/total/amount_paid, status draft/unpaid/partially_paid/paid/cancelled, created/posted by+at; indexes delivery+status, SO+status) and `sales_payments` (RCP-{year}-{id}, mirrors purchase_payments).
- Enum `SalesInvoiceStatus`; models `SalesInvoice` (`balance()`, `isOverdue()`, `ensureIsDraft()`, `syncPaymentStatus()`), `SalesPayment`; `SalesDelivery::salesInvoices()/liveInvoice()`, `SalesOrder::salesInvoices()`; 3 new `AccountSystemRole`s **AccountsReceivable (Piutang Usaha), SalesRevenue (Penjualan), VatOut (PPN Keluaran)** — now 21 roles; exceptions `InvalidSalesInvoiceException`, `InvalidSalesPaymentException`.
- Actions `app/Actions/Sales/`: `SaveSalesInvoiceDraftAction` (+ static `checkDates`, `lines`, `totals`, `chargesBilledElsewhere`), `PostSalesInvoiceAction` (+ `preview`, `plan`), `CancelSalesInvoiceAction`, `RecordSalesPaymentAction` (+ `reservedRoles`, `plan`), `CancelSalesPaymentAction`.
- Gates `sales.invoice` (Accounting, Manager) and `sales.invoice.view` (+ Sales). Morph keys `sales_invoice`, `sales_payment`. Routes `accounting.sales-invoices.list/.create/{salesDelivery}/.edit/.show`.
- Pages `accounting/sales-invoices/`: `⚡list` (tabs "Pengiriman belum difakturkan" / "Faktur", outstanding receivables tile), `⚡form` (invoice date, due date from terms, PPN %; goods table read-only; order charges card says whether they're on this invoice), `⚡show` (tiles, lines, customer payments, summary, journal links for `journal.manage` only; post / cancel / record payment / cancel payment dialogs with journal previews, all behind `sales.invoice`). SO show page, Pengiriman tab: each posted delivery shows its invoice number + status (link for `sales.invoice.view`) or "belum difakturkan". Sidebar (Akuntansi) "Faktur Penjualan". 43 `lang/id.json` keys. ERD updated (both tables rewritten to match, rules as comments).

**Verified (all rolled back):** logic 21/21 on SO-2026-000019 delivered 20 + 30 (5% discount, 10.000 charge): invoice 1 DPP 960.000 / invoice 2 DPP 1.435.000 as drafts (both showed the charge); posting invoice 2 first kept the charge, invoice 1 recalculated to 950.000 at posting; Σ DPP = 2.385.000 = SO total; journal Dr Piutang 1.592.850 / Cr Penjualan 1.435.000 / Cr PPN Keluaran 157.850; partial → paid; overpay, receive into Piutang, cancel with payments, second invoice per delivery, future date, before delivery, due before invoice all refused; cancelling the invoice with the charge → re-invoice picks the charge up again; 0% PPN → 2 journal lines. Pages 17/17 incl. due date following the invoice date (NET 7), post blocked while PPN Keluaran unmapped, Sales 403 on the list and on every action (even called directly) but can view, SO page invoice badge. Screenshots checked (form, show with partial payment, list).

**Before using it in the app:** map the 3 new roles on Daftar Akun — Piutang Usaha (Harta), Penjualan (Pendapatan), PPN Keluaran (Kewajiban). Test-run ID gaps were reset (`ALTER TABLE … AUTO_INCREMENT = 1` snaps to max id + 1).

Laravel sorts all migration files by filename across folders, and the timestamps already run in dependency order. Two fixes were needed:

The auto foreign key name `purchase_order_receipt_attachments_purchase_order_receipt_batch_id_foreign` is over MySQL's 64-character limit. Both now use `constrained(indexName: 'po_receipt_attachments_batch_fk')`. Before this, a fresh install had never worked past this file.
